Theorem 218.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let ρ be a convergence analysis functional on the wave space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 469 and the convergence rate is O(881/n^2).

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the wave space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 469 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 881. QED.
